- **function-audit**: Owner-triggered function audit of FlexNotes. One function-auditor agent per area reads every source file in full, follows every function to its result against the data matrix and the owner's imperatives, and records it in the docs/audit ledgers. It changes no app code; it ends by putting every action needed to resolve its findings into GitHub issues, the tracker and the roadmap. Run it when the owner types /function-audit or asks for an audit of working order. Builds don't run this: they use the targeted build-audit gate. ([davendra/fundadmin-ai-skills](https://github.com/davendra/fundadmin-ai-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/fundadmin/skills/fund-review-gp-update))

Use when the user runs `/fund review-k1`, asks to check a K-1, or shares a K-1 tax package. Writes `K1-REVIEW-[fund]-[date].md`. ([davendra/fundadmin-ai-skills](https://github.com/davendra/fundadmin-ai-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/fundadmin/skills/fund-review-k1))
- **fund-review-lpa**: Runs the 5-agent LPA pipeline (terms, risks, compliance, obligations, recommendations) into methodology v2 heuristic document favourability, or an explicit unavailable result. Use when the user runs `/fund review-lpa`, asks to review fund terms, or shares an LPA. Writes `LPA-REVIEW-[fund]-[date].md`. ([davendra/fundadmin-ai-skills](https://github.com/davendra/fundadmin-ai-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/fundadmin/skills/fund-review-lpa))
- **fund-review-poa**: Reviews a Power of Attorney used in fund work: classifies it as investor/LPA or transactional, extracts grantor, attorneys and authority, rates breadth of discretion, and lists cross-border questions for counsel. Use when the user runs `/fund review-poa` or shares a POA or mandate. Writes POA-REVIEW-[fund]-[date].md. - **fund-review-ppm**: Reviews a PPM or offering memorandum for risk factor completeness, fee disclosure clarity, stated regulatory status (Reg D, 3(c)(1)/3(c)(7), ERISA) and LPA consistency, as counts. Use when the user runs `/fund review-ppm`, asks to review offering documents, or shares a PPM. Writes `PPM-REVIEW-[fund]-[date].md`. - **fund-review-side-letter**: Builds an obligation inventory from a side letter, categorizes every right, rates MFN cascade risk, and flags LPA and cross-side-letter conflicts.
